# Duel core: documented functions (`duel_core`)

Reference table generated in the documentation round of 2026-10-31. Each row is a function that reached the `documented` stage (`docs/PROGRESS.md`): its prototype, purpose, return value, side effects and callers, read from the Ghidra decompilation with the names of that round. The same text is the function's plate comment in Ghidra (`re/documented*.tsv`, applied by `tools/ghidra/ApplySignatures.java`). How the subsystem works as a whole is in [`../duel_core.md`](../duel_core.md); pointers to structures are typed `void *` and the structure is named in the summary.

Functions: 870 (rel_duel_draw 72, rel_duel_eng 792, rel_duel_mgr 6).


## `rel_duel_draw`

| Address | Prototype | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0x0989d4c8` | `void duel_core_init_view_side_map(void)` | Builds the side maps of the draw module so that the local side is view side 0: with l = thunk_duel_core_is_local_side(1), sets the engine-to-view map 0x098e9aa0 = (l != 0), 0x098e9aa4 = (l == 0), and copies them to the view-to-engine map 0x098e9aa8, 0x098e9aac. No return value. Side effects: the four map words. Callers: 1 (ui_duel_draw_begin). |

| `0x09de1d70` | `int duel_core_standby_queue_lv_monster_triggers(int side)` | Standby check: side's monster zones 0-4 whose duel_core_get_slot_card_id is one of 0x1add, 0x1adc, 0x1ad7, 0x1ad6, 0x1817, 0x1814, 0x1812, 0x17d9, 0x17d5, 0x185e, 0x1822, 0x17d1 (the LV and similar monsters listed in the naming notes), face-up (u16 +0x08) with flags bit 27 clear, get duel_core_chain_queue_field_trigger(side, slot, 2 = Standby Phase, 0); at the first one leaving the trigger count 0x09e28e48 non-zero sets slot flag bit 27 (duel_core_post_set_slot_flag(side, slot, 0x1b, 1)) and returns 0. | `0x09de1ed4` | `int duel_core_standby_queue_marked_instance_trigger(int side, int card_id)` | Standby check: walks side's player slot record list (head u16 +0x11a, pool 0x09e2c7cc) for kind-1 or kind-2 records with key card_id and counter (byte +2 high nibble) 0; for each, locates the card instance held in its value u16 +4 (duel_core_find_card_location) and queues duel_core_chain_queue_trigger(card_id \| side << 31 \| 0x4400000 \| location << 16, instance, 0) (selector 2, event 2); once the trigger count 0x09e28e48 is non-zero clears that record (duel_core_post_clear_marker_with_value(side, 11, card_id, instance)) and returns 0. | `0x09de23f4` | `int duel_core_filter_tribute_other_own_monster(int side, int location, int index)` | Selection filter: with slot = location + index, returns 0x800 when slot is a monster zone (< 5), side is the turn player (0x09e2d1d8), duel_core_can_tribute_for_effect(side, side, slot) holds and slot differs from the loop word 0x09e2d21c (the slot being processed); otherwise 0. No side effects. No direct caller (passed as a selection-filter callback). |
| `0x09de3b78` | `int duel_core_step_turn_end(void)` | Last turn step (table 0x09e01da0), sub-step 0x09e2d214. Sub-step 0: posts command 3 (end-of-turn cleanup) for the turn side 0x09e2d1d8. Sub-step 1: in duel mode 2 (0x09e2d1f4) when the turn counter 0x09e2d1e0 equals 0x09e2d1e4 calls duel_core_declare_turn_limit_loss(local side); else when the counter equals 0x09e2d1e4 + 3 and the two sides' LP (block +0x00) differ, decides the duel for the side with more LP: result 0x09e2d1f0 = 1 when that is the local side (0x09e02994), else 2, win reason 0x0b in that side's +0x30 and in 0x09e02cb4, 0x09e2d1d4 = 1; otherwise posts command 4 (next turn). Always returns 0. Side effects: posted commands, the duel-result globals. No direct caller (turn step table 0x09e01da0). |
| `0x09de3d40` | `int duel_core_net_run_peer_target_choice(void)` | Link-duel request run for the peer, state 0x09e289ac: 0 clears 0x09e28f6c, 0x09e28f70, 0x09e28f74 and goes to 1; 1 sets 0x09e28e44 to the link at 0x09e28cb4 + 0x09e28e38 x 0x18 and calls duel_effects_call_handler_14(link, previous) (previous = the link 0x18 bytes lower, null when 0x09e28e38 < 1 or its card id is 0); a non-zero result stores (result > 0) in bit 0 of link byte +4 and advances to 2; returns 0. State 2 sends the 0x18-byte link with duel_core_net_send_command(0x19, -1, link, 0x18) and returns 1; any other state returns 1. Side effects: the state word, the link's +4 bit 0, 0x09e28e44, the network packet. Callers: 1 (duel_core_net_run_peer_link_requests). |
| `0x09de3e5c` | `int duel_core_net_run_peer_resolve(void)` | Link-duel request run for the peer, state 0x09e289b0: 0 clears 0x09e28f6c, 0x09e28f70, 0x09e28f64, 0x09e28f68, sets 0x09e28f60 = 0x80, calls duel_core_clear_deck_shuffle_flags and goes to 1; 1 sets 0x09e28e44 to the link at 0x09e28cb4 + 0x09e28e38 x 0x18 and stores duel_effects_resolve(link, previous) in 0x09e28f60 (previous = the link 0x18 bytes lower, null when 0x09e28e38 < 1), advancing to 2 when it returns 0; returns 0. State 2 sends duel_core_net_send_command(0x17, -1, ...) with the link (0x18 bytes) or, when a previous link exists, both links (0x30 bytes from the previous one) and returns 1; other states return 1. Side effects: the state word, 0x09e28f60-0x09e28f70, 0x09e28e44, player flags bit 3, those of duel_effects_resolve, the packet. Callers: 1 (duel_core_net_run_peer_link_requests). |
| `0x09de3f88` | `int duel_core_net_run_peer_link_requests(void)` | Link duel: runs one pending peer request per call. When the activation-step flag 0x09e2898c is set it works on the chain link at index 0x09e28e38 (0x09e28cb4 + index x 0x18; previous link or NULL): state 0x09e289a8 0 clears 0x09e28f6c/0x09e28f74 and goes to 1; 1 makes it the current link (0x09e28e44) and calls duel_effects_call_activation_step(link, previous), and on a non-zero result sets link byte +3 bit 7 when positive and advances; 2 sends the 24-byte link to the peer (duel_core_net_send_command(0x1b, -1, link, 0x18)) and clears the flag. Otherwise it runs, in order, the first set request: 0x09e28984 duel_core_net_run_peer_target_choice, 0x09e28970 duel_core_net_answer_response_window, 0x09e28994 duel_core_net_run_peer_resolve, clearing that flag when the worker returns non-zero. Returns 0 when nothing was pending, else 1. Side effects: the request flags and state, the link, the packet send. Callers: 1 (duel_core_poll_net_peer_requests). |
| `0x09de413c` | `int duel_core_poll_net_peer_requests(void)` | Runs duel_core_net_run_peer_link_requests() (the requests the local side runs for the link-duel peer) and returns 0. Side effects: those of duel_core_net_run_peer_link_requests. Callers: 1 (duel_core_run_turn_flow). |

## `rel_duel_mgr`

| Address | Prototype | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0x08ea9cd4` | `int duel_core_is_local_default_rules_duel(void)` | Returns 1 for a duel that is not a network duel (thunk_system_get_net_active_flag() == 0) and whose set-up uses the default rules (duel_core_setup_has_default_rules: LP mode +0x196 and time limit +0x197 both 0); otherwise 0. No side effects. Callers: 2 (ui_duel_result_init, ui_duel_result_draw_summary_page). |
| `0x08ea9d14` | `int duel_core_setup_has_default_rules(void)` | Returns 1 when the duel set-up block (thunk_deck_get_duel_setup_buffer()) has LP mode +0x196 == 0 (8000 LP) and time limit +0x197 == 0, otherwise 0. No side effects. Callers: 2 (duel_core_is_local_default_rules_duel, ui_duel_result_init). |
| `0x08ea9ef0` | `int duel_core_get_setup_start_lp(int side)` | Returns the starting Life Points from the duel set-up block (thunk_deck_get_duel_setup_buffer()) LP mode byte +0x196: 1 -> 2000, 2 -> 4000, 3 -> 12000, 4 -> 16000, 5 -> the custom value u32 +0x198 (side 0) or +0x19c (side != 0), any other value -> 8000. No side effects. Callers: 1 (duel_core_setup_engine_for_duel). |
| `0x08ea9f68` | `bool duel_core_is_player_on_second_side(void)` | Scans the duellist records (0x1ac bytes each at +0x1a4 of the duel set-up buffer from thunk_deck_get_duel_setup_buffer; 2 records, or 4 when byte +0x192 is 2 = tag) for character u16 +0x00 == 1 (the player): records 0 and 2 set bit 0, records 1 and 3 set bit 1. Returns true when only bit 1 is set (the player duels only on the second side). No side effects. Callers: 1 (duel_core_setup_engine_for_duel). |
| `0x08eaa878` | `void duel_core_reset_turn_timers(void)` | Resets the four duelist timers (the first four ints of the duel session block returned by thunk_deck_get_duel_setup_tail_block, +0x00..+0x0c) to limit - 1, where the limit comes from the duel set-up buffer (thunk_deck_get_duel_setup_buffer): 0 when mode byte +0x190 is 2, otherwise by time-limit byte +0x197: 1 -> 900, 2 -> 1800 (0x708), 3 -> 5400 (0x1518), 4 -> 9000, else 0 (so -1 = no limit). No return value. Side effects: the four timer words. Callers: 3 (tag_duel_handle_partner_swap, duel_core_setup_engine_for_duel, system_duel_mgr_tutorial_reset_callback). |
| `0x08eab240` | `void duel_core_tick_turn_timers(void)` | rel_duel_mgr, every frame: decrements the per-duelist turn timers (words thunk_deck_get_duel_setup_tail_block() + duelist x 4). Offline: for each side, duelist d = thunk_duel_core_map_side_to_duelist(side); when the u16 at set-up buffer +0x1a4 + d x 0x1ac (the duelist record's first u16) is below 2 and the side is awaiting input (thunk_duel_core_is_awaiting_player_input), a positive timer is decremented; then the first of the four duelists whose timer is 0 loses (thunk_duel_core_declare_turn_limit_loss(1) for duelists 1 and 3, (0) for 0 and 2). In a link duel (thunk_system_get_net_active_flag) only duelists whose record u16 +0x0c equals the local peer index are ticked, a timer at 0 declares the loss, and every 30 frames (0x08eba020 % 30) the timer is broadcast (thunk_system_net_api_broadcast_duelist_value). No return value. Side effects: the timers, the loss declaration, the broadcast. Callers: 1 (system_duel_mgr_frame_callback). |
